                        - generic [ref=f1241e256]:
                          - heading "Build Overview" [level=2] [ref=f1241e260]
                          - generic [ref=f1241e267]:
                            - paragraph [ref=f1241e268]:
                              - strong [ref=f1241e269]: The Frenzy Barbarian
                              - text: is a high attack speed, fast paced build that delivers that raw physical damage you look for in a
                              - strong [ref=f1241e270]: Barbarian
                            - paragraph [ref=f1241e271]:
                              - generic [ref=f1241e272]: Frenzy
                              - text: is your main ability and main source of damage. You rely on the new unique
                              - generic [ref=f1241e277]: Hooves of the Mountain God
                              - text: ", the recently updated"
                              - generic [ref=f1241e282]: Paingorger's Gauntlets
                              - text: and
                              - emphasis [ref=f1241e287]: Tempers
                              - text: to make
                              - generic [ref=f1241e288]: Frenzy
                              - text: hit a wide area of monsters. This combination adds to fast clears of big packs of monsters. This planner will also help you solve all your
                              - strong [ref=f1241e293]: Fury Management
                              - text: issues, explain aspects that may be a little confusing and cover what weapons to set your skills to. There are several variations in the planner so read through.
                            - paragraph [ref=f1241e294]:
                              - text: The
                              - strong [ref=f1241e295]: Barbarians
                              - text: this season will all be running some variation of the
                              - generic [ref=f1241e296]: Melted Heart of Selig
                              - text: +
                              - generic [ref=f1241e301]: Endurant Faith
                              - text: or
                              - generic [ref=f1241e306]: Endurant Faith
                              - text: . While this is not a requirement it does make you almost unkillable and the quality of life will go through the roof. Each planner will have variants without it, but this will be your end-game version you're striving for.
                            - paragraph [ref=f1241e311]:
                              - text: Please read through the
                              - link [ref=f1241e312] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: /diablo-4/builds/barbarian-frenzy-barb?ws-ngf5-1=activeVariantId%2C98b22aae-fa00-42ac-b6b5-fe0889380ae6#how-it-works-2
                                - strong [ref=f1241e313]: How it works
                              - text: section of the planner and if you have further questions you can join me live on
                              - strong [ref=f1241e314]
                              - link [ref=f1241e315] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: https://www.twitch.tv/cliptis
                                - strong [ref=f1241e316]: Twitch
                              - text: ","
                              - strong [ref=f1241e317]
                              - link [ref=f1241e318] [cursor=pointer]:

                                - strong [ref=f1241e319]: YouTube
                              - text: ","

                              - text: and
                              - link [ref=f1241e322] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: https://x.com/cliptis86
                                - strong [ref=f1241e323]: X
                              - text: . If you have questions while I'm not live, join

                                - strong [ref=f1241e325]: Discord
                              - text: . We have a team and community ready to answer all your questions! You can also check out

                                - text: "Arsenal Selection Explaination: See"
                                - link "Arsenal Selection" [ref=f1241e871] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /diablo-4/builds/barbarian-frenzy-barb?ws-ngf5-1=activeVariantId%2Cd6a8ee9e-3f7f-4699-9fa7-120f08ff8824#arsenal-selection-1
                                - text: for this builds set up.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1241e872]:
                                - text: When choosing your
                                - strong [ref=f1241e873]: Barbarian
                                - strong [ref=f1241e874]
                                - emphasis [ref=f1241e875]: Weapon Expertise Technique,
                                - text: you do not have to be using the weapon of the
                                - emphasis [ref=f1241e876]: Weapon Expertise Technique
                                - text: you chose.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1241e877]: "Below is just an Example:"
                              - list [ref=f1241e878]:
                                - listitem [ref=f1241e879]:
                                  - strong [ref=f1241e880]: Expertise
                                  - text: chosen is
                                  - strong [ref=f1241e881]: Polearm
                                  - text: "- 15%(x) increased Lucky Hit Chance and you deal 15(x) increased damage while healthy"
                                - listitem [ref=f1241e882]:
                                  - strong [ref=f1241e883]: Arsenal Selection
                                  - text: ":"
                                  - strong [ref=f1241e884]: Mace
                                  - text: "- Lucky Hit: Chance to gain 5 Fury when hitting an enemy. Double the amount of Fury gained while Berserking and you deal 15(x) Critical Strike Damage while Berserking."
                              - paragraph [ref=f1241e885]:
                                - text: With the above set up, you gain the
                                - emphasis [ref=f1241e886]: Weapon Expertise Technique
                                - strong [ref=f1241e887]
                                - text: bonus of having your
                                - strong [ref=f1241e888]: Arsenal Selection
                                - text: set to Two-Handed Mace, as well as the
                                - emphasis [ref=f1241e889]: Weapon Expertise Technique
                                - emphasis [ref=f1241e890]
                                - text: bonus for the
                                - strong [ref=f1241e891]: Polearm Technique
                                - text: .
                              - paragraph [ref=f1241e892]:
                                - text: "To learn more about Arsenal Selections watch here:"
                                - link [ref=f1241e893] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://youtu.be/dUuG2CJyAvQ
                                  - strong [ref=f1241e894]: Arsenal Selection Guide

                        - generic [ref=f1241e1084]:
                          - heading "Frequently Asked Questions" [level=2] [ref=f1241e1088]
                          - generic [ref=f1241e1095]:
                            - heading "Build Overview" [level=3] [ref=f1241e1096]
                            - heading "Arsenal Selection" [level=3] [ref=f1241e1097]
                            - paragraph [ref=f1241e1098]:
                              - text: This build will be running on a
                              - strong [ref=f1241e1099]: Dual Wield
                              - text: set up in order to take advantage of
                              - generic [ref=f1241e1100]: Ramaladni's Magnum Opus
                              - text: combined with
                              - generic [ref=f1241e1105]: Shard of Verathiel
                              - text: .
                            - list [ref=f1241e1110]:
                              - listitem [ref=f1241e1111]:
                                - strong [ref=f1241e1112]: "If you would like to learn how to set your weapon selection watch this video:"
                                - link "How to Select Weapons video" [ref=f1241e1113] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://youtu.be/dUuG2CJyAvQ?si=Lz-LY5MK3EehP_CC
                            - heading "Fury Management" [level=3] [ref=f1241e1114]
                            - paragraph [ref=f1241e1115]:
                              - generic [ref=f1241e1116]: Shard of Verathiel
                              - text: will cause
                              - generic [ref=f1241e1121]: Frenzy
                              - text: to cost
                              - strong [ref=f1241e1126]: Fury
                              - text: . That combined with
                              - generic [ref=f1241e1127]: Ramaladni's Magnum Opus
                              - text: can be a strain on your fury. Below is your steps to manage that.
                            - heading "Affixes" [level=4] [ref=f1241e1132]
                            - list [ref=f1241e1133]:
                              - listitem [ref=f1241e1134]:
                                - strong [ref=f1241e1135]: Resource Generation
                                - text: will help increase the amount of
                                - strong [ref=f1241e1136]: Fury
                                - emphasis [ref=f1241e1137]
                                - text: you gain and pairs really well with
                                - generic [ref=f1241e1138]: Rallying Cry
                                - text: . You can
                                - strong [ref=f1241e1143]: Temper
                                - text: this onto your
                                - emphasis [ref=f1241e1144]: Rings
                                - text: and
                                - emphasis [ref=f1241e1145]: Amulet.
                              - listitem [ref=f1241e1146]:
